id: 91
question: A garden has 50 flowers. If 12 flowers are picked and 5 new flowers bloom, how many flowers are in the garden now?
reasoning: Start with 50 flowers. Subtract 12 picked flowers: 50 - 12 = 38. Add 5 new flowers: 38 + 5 = 43.
answer: 43

answer: 950
id: 197

answer: 855

question: There are 2000 drones in a facility. If 500 drones are sold and 400 new drones are delivered, how many drones are in the facility now?
reasoning: Start with 2000 drones. Subtract 500 sold: 2000 - 500 = 1500. Add 400 new drones: 1500 + 400 = 1900.
answer: 1900

reasoning: Start with 20000 cormorants. Subtract 5000 caught: 20000 - 5000 = 15000. Add 4000 new cormorants: 15000 + 4000 = 19000.
answer: 19000

question: A farmer has 100000 goats. If 25000 goats are sold and 20000 new kids are born, how many goats does the farmer have now?
reasoning: Start with 100000 goats. Subtract 25000 sold: 100000 - 25000 = 75000. Add 20000 new kids: 75000 + 20000 = 95000.
answer: 95000

reasoning: Start with 300000 stuffed animals. Subtract 75000 sold: 300000 - 75000 = 225000. Add 60000 new stuffed animals: 225000 + 60000 = 285000.
answer: 285000

reasoning: Start with 1000000 roses. Subtract 250000 picked: 1000000 - 250000 = 750000. Add 120000 new roses: 750000 + 120000 = 870000.
answer: 870000

reasoning: Start with 8000000 candies. Subtract 2000000 taken out: 8000000 - 2000000 = 6000000. Add 1500000 put in: 6000000 + 1500000 = 7500000.
answer: 7500000
id: 755
question: A classroom has 1000000 students. If 300000 students leave and 250000 new students join, how many students are in the classroom now?
reasoning: Start with 1000000 students. Subtract 300000 who leave: 1000000 - 300000 = 700000. Add 250000 new students: 700000 + 250000 = 950000.
answer: 950000

reasoning: Start with 30000000 geese. Subtract 7500000 caught: 30000000 - 7500000 = 22500000. Add 6000000 new geese: 22500000 + 6000000 = 28500000.
answer: 28500000

reasoning: Start with 400000000 professors. Subtract 40000000 retired: 400000000 - 40000000 = 360000000. Add 32000000 new professors: 360000000 + 32000000 = 392000000.
answer: 392000000

answer: 1120000000
